    ---
    title: use client
    description: Learn how to use the use client directive to render a component on the client.
    ---
    
    The `'use client'` directive declares an entry point for the components to be rendered on the **client side** and should be used when creating interactive user interfaces (UI) that require client-side JavaScript capabilities, such as state management, event handling, and access to browser APIs. This is a React feature.
    
    > **Good to know:**
    >
    > You do not need to add the `'use client'` directive to every file that contains Client Components. You only need to add it to the files whose components you want to render directly within Server Components. The `'use client'` directive defines the [server and client boundary](/docs/app/guides/server-and-client-boundary), and the components exported from such a file serve as entry points to the client.
    
    ## Usage
    
    To declare an entry point for the Client Components, add the `'use client'` directive **at the top of the file**, before any imports. The directive applies to the entire module and cannot be placed inside a component or function:
    
    ```tsx filename="app/components/counter.tsx" highlight={1} switcher
    'use client'
    
    import { useState } from 'react'
    
    export default function Counter() {
      const [count, setCount] = useState(0)
    
      return (
        <div>
          <p>Count: {count}</p>
          <button onClick={() => setCount(count + 1)}>Increment</button>
        </div>
      )
    }
    ```
    
    ```jsx filename="app/components/counter.js" highlight={1} switcher
    'use client'
    
    import { useState } from 'react'
    
    export default function Counter() {
      const [count, setCount] = useState(0)
    
      return (
        <div>
          <p>Count: {count}</p>
          <button onClick={() => setCount(count + 1)}>Increment</button>
        </div>
      )
    }
    ```
    
    When using the `'use client'` directive, the props of the Client Components must be [serializable](https://react.dev/reference/rsc/use-client#serializable-types). This means the props need to be in a format that React can serialize when sending data from the server to the client.
    
    ```tsx filename="app/components/counter.tsx" highlight={4} switcher
    'use client'
    
    export default function Counter({
      onClick /* ❌ Function is not serializable */,
    }) {
      return (
        <div>
          <button onClick={onClick}>Increment</button>
        </div>
      )
    }
    ```
    
    ```jsx filename="app/components/counter.js" highlight={4} switcher
    'use client'
    
    export default function Counter({
      onClick /* ❌ Function is not serializable */,
    }) {
      return (
        <div>
          <button onClick={onClick}>Increment</button>
        </div>
      )
    }
    ```
    
    ## Nesting Client Components within Server Components
    
    Combining Server and Client Components allows you to build applications that are both performant and interactive:
    
    1. **Server Components**: Use for static content, data fetching, and SEO-friendly elements.
    2. **Client Components**: Use for interactive elements that require state, effects, or browser APIs.
    3. **Component composition**: Nest Client Components within Server Components as needed for a clear separation of server and client logic.
    
    In the following example:
    
    - `Header` is a Server Component handling static content.
    - `Counter` is a Client Component enabling interactivity within the page.
    
    ```tsx filename="app/page.tsx" highlight={2,8} switcher
    import Header from './header'
    import Counter from './counter' // This is a Client Component
    
    export default function Page() {
      return (
        <div>
          <Header />
          <Counter />
        </div>
      )
    }
    ```
    
    ```jsx filename="app/page.js" highlight={2,8} switcher
    import Header from './header'
    import Counter from './counter' // This is a Client Component
    
    export default function Page() {
      return (
        <div>
          <Header />
          <Counter />
        </div>
      )
    }
    ```
    
    ✓ • 0ms

    $ sed -n '1,115p' /home/csanchez/.codex/plugins/cache/openai-curated-remote/sites/1.1.7/skills/sites/SKILL.md && sed -n '78,126p' docs/DESIGN.md && tail -105 PLAN.md
    ---
    name: sites
    description: Use Sites when the user wants a website built for them (including a landing page, portfolio, dashboard, portal, tracker, hub, game, event registration, or tool), requests changes to a website already built with Sites, wants to publish a website with Sites, or wants to manage Sites hosting.
    ---
    
    # Sites
    
    Take the requested website through the supported development and publishing workflow. Publish completed new Sites and edits unless the user requests local-only work or saving without deployment. New Sites start private; preserve an existing Site's audience unless the user requests a change. Native tool approvals apply without an additional conversational publishing gate.
    
    For hosting-only changes, use the relevant native Sites tool and confirm its returned state; source preparation is needed only for source changes or publication.
    
    ## Workflow
    
    `<plugin-root>` is the installed directory containing `scripts/` and `skills/`. Run helpers with absolute paths and literal arguments from the selected checkout. Keep credentials in session memory and pass them through stdin, never shell arguments, files, or output.
    
    ### 1. Select and open
    
    Default to a new Site unless a specific existing Site is identified.
    
    - **Existing hosted Site:** retain its `project_id` and audience, call `get_site` and `create_source_repository_write_credential`, and open with the [source helper](#source-helper) before editing. Omit `archivePath`; use an empty directory when source is missing, then the returned `checkout_path`.
    - **New Site:** create a unique, new empty project subdirectory in the task's workspace, normally `/workspace/sites/<slug>` in managed Linux. Default simple pages and browser-local interactions to buildless HTML/JS; use the supplied Vinext starter for server behavior. If the user requests another framework or implementation, or the task specifies a template to reuse, skip the Vinext starter and build directly with that choice. For hosting, follow the minimum deploy format under [Hosting manifest](#hosting-manifest).
    - **Local-only:** use the available checkout; skip registration, source synchronization, and publication.
    
    ### 2. Prepare and launch
    
    For new Vinext projects, run:
    
    ```text
    node <plugin-root>/scripts/project-setup.mjs
    ```
    
    This copies the starter and configures ignored checkout-local state. Helpers select `managed-linux` only for `SITES_MANAGED_LINUX_CONTAINER=1`, otherwise `portable`. For buildless HTML/JS, prepare `dist/` for `index.html` and assets; skip starter setup, installation, and build.
    
    For a retained template, add `--template-source <absolute-sanitized-source-directory>`; exclude Site identity, Git metadata, credentials, and runtime data. For explicitly requested Worker ESM, add `--starter worker-esm` and follow [its README](templates/worker-esm-starter/README.md). Use [Troubleshooting](references/troubleshooting.md#project-configuration) for profile repair or static-to-Worker migration.
    
    After minimal setup, launch registration for new hosted work in a retained execution cell when available. Native `create_site` creates a private, unpublished Site and returns its ID and source credential. Immediately persist the ID when it returns. For static Sites, include `--static-directory` when known to set both fields at once; omit it for Worker builds. For buildless HTML/JS, run:
    
    ```text
    node <plugin-root>/scripts/set-project-id.mjs --project-id <returned-id> --static-directory dist
    ```
    
    Reuse an existing ID or registration attempt; resolve uncertain creation before retrying. For app integrations, first read [discovery and declarations](references/app-integrations.md#discover-and-declare).
    
    Start needed dependency installation before feature implementation:
    
    ```text
    node <plugin-root>/scripts/install-dependencies.mjs
    ```
    
    Run only when dependencies are missing or their inputs changed. The installer handles host settings, package-manager selection, and interrupted setup; it takes no flags. Use one installer; keep dependency inputs unchanged while it runs. Proceed to implementation once the applicable operations are launched.
    
    For managed-image pnpm additions, use `node "$SITES_PNPM_BIN" add <package>`.
    
    Start required image search or generation when its brief is clear, alongside independent source work. Consider a parallel subagent for image downloads. Delegate bounded asset or research tasks; the Site owner handles the checkout, native Sites calls in the owning conversation, and publication.
    
    ### 3. Implement
    
    Write application files while setup runs. For hosted work, await persisted Site identity before further manifest edits or source synchronization. Await installed dependencies before commands that need them.
    
    Implement the requested experience in one focused pass; revise for observed failures or unmet requirements. Replace starter title and description and remove temporary `codex-preview` metadata during implementation. Integrate required assets and complete the requested behavior before publishing.
    
    When `SITES_MANAGED_LINUX_CONTAINER=1`, preview is internal QA. Start it only when the task needs browser QA and `cua_repl.js` is available. If `cua_repl.js` is unavailable, skip browser QA: do not start a preview server, install a browser, or improvise another browser-control path.
    
    When `SITES_MANAGED_LINUX_CONTAINER` is not `1`, use local preview. In visible tasks with user-facing preview support, show the first recognizable version once it serves successfully and keep it current. Background tasks skip user-facing handoff.
    
    Before any preview, browser test, or screenshot, read the selected reference: [managed](references/preview/managed.md) or [local](references/preview/local.md). If preview infrastructure is unavailable, report the verification limit and continue appropriate checks and publication unless passing browser QA is required. Follow [Troubleshooting](references/troubleshooting.md#preview) after failures.
    
    ### 4. Validate and package
    
    Collect any remaining registration and installation results, then finalize the source manifest before checks/builds and packaging.
    
    #### Hosting manifest
    
    The tracked `.openai/hosting.json` declares Site identity and deployment configuration. After registration, a minimal static Site uses:
    
    ```json
    {
      "project_id": "<returned-id>",
      "static": {"directory": "dist"}
    }
    ```
    
    | Field | Contract |
    | --- | --- |
    | `project_id` | Persist with `set-project-id.mjs` on creation; preserve on updates. |
    | `static` | Object with `directory`: `dist`, `dist/client`, `out`, `build`, or `.output/public`. Omit for Worker builds. |
    | `d1`, `r2` | [Storage bindings](references/storage.md#bindings); leave unused bindings `null`. |
    | `capabilities` | Declare [a Site MCP server](references/site-mcp-server.md#build-the-server) when needed. |
    | `plugins`, `connectors` | [App integration declarations](references/app-integrations.md#discover-and-declare). |
    
    A deployable Site needs `.openai/hosting.json` with its `project_id` and one of these build outputs:
    
    - **Static:** Set `static.directory` to a supported directory listed above. It must contain `index.html` and the assets the page uses.
    - **Server-backed:** Omit `static`. Build a Cloudflare Worker ES module at `dist/server/index.js` that default-exports an object with a `fetch` handler.
    
    Preserve existing fields. Edit the source manifest; build helpers emit Worker deployment metadata.
    
    Run checks appropriate to the change; lint only when requested. For hosted work, package with the source helper using remaining checks/builds as ordered argument arrays and an absolute `archivePath`. Pass the complete prior opening result as `source` when available.
    
    Buildless HTML/JS uses `commands: []` when no checks remain. Framework projects, including Vinext and static exports, build with `["node", "<plugin-root>/scripts/build-site.mjs"]`. Omit commands already completed successfully with unchanged inputs. Local-only work runs checks/builds directly and skips packaging for publication.
    
    #### Source helper
    
    Use the repository access from registration or `create_source_repository_write_credential` for the same Site. If `auth_mode` is `work_proxy`, it contains no token. Use the same helper or ordinary Git with `remote_url`; Work supplies authentication outside the executor. If Git reports expired access, call `create_source_repository_write_credential` again. Do not configure a token or authentication header for this mode.
    
    ```text
    node <plugin-root>/scripts/site-workflow.mjs --project-id <project_id>
    ```
    
    Launch with `exec_command(tty: true, yield_time_ms: 1000)`. After `Ready for Site workflow JSON on stdin (input is hidden).`, send one newline-terminated JSON object with `write_stdin`. Wait for successful exit and retain the final JSON result.
    
    | Input | Value |
    | --- | --- |
    | `credential` | Returned credential object, through stdin only. |
    | `source` | Complete prior opening result, when available. |
    - **Financial numbers:** 24–28 px / weight 650; tabular numeral feature.
    - **Buttons / nav:** 13–14 px / 500–600.
    - **Never:** all-caps paragraphs or decorative, heavy display fonts.
    
    ### Geometry and spacing
    
    - **Spacing unit:** 4 px. Preferred gaps: 8, 12, 16, 20, 24, 32, 40 px.
    - **Sidebar:** 248 px expanded on desktop, collapsible to a 64 px icon rail; subtle right border. Collapse state is temporary. Below 1024 px, use a modal slide-over menu.
    - **Top bar:** 80 px high, aligned with the expanded sidebar header; breadcrumb/location on left, active roommate switcher on right.
    - **Main max width:** 1120 px with 40 px horizontal interior padding desktop, 20 px tablet, 16 px phone.
    - **Card radius:** 10 px; **controls radius:** 7 px; **modal radius:** 12 px.
    - **Buttons:** min height 36 px desktop; tap targets >=44 px on touch layouts.
    - **Borders:** 1 px subtle divider; low contrast. **Shadows:** almost none (optional small dialog shadow only).
    - **Tables:** row height approx 44–52 px; hover tint, sparse column dividers.
    
    ## 4. Application shell and navigation
    
    ### Desktop (>=1024 px)
    
    ```text
    ┌────────────────────┬──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
    │ R  RoomieOS        │ Maple House / Dashboard           [Avery ▾]             │
    │                    ├──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
    │ ⌂  Dashboard       │                                                          │
    │ ☑  Chores          │  🏠 Maple House                                          │
    │ ◫  Expenses        │  A calmer way to share your space.                       │
    │ ▤  Agreements      │                                                          │
    │                    │  [My chores] [My balance] [Open expenses]               │
    │                    │                                                          │
    │ ─────────────────  │  Upcoming chores                  Household snapshot      │
    │ ⚙  Settings        │  ┌─────────────────────────┐      ┌───────────────────┐   │
    │                    │  │ Clean kitchen ...       │      │ 5 pending chores  │   │
    │ Local demo         │  │ Take out trash ...      │      │ 2 open expenses   │   │
    │ Saved on browser   │  └─────────────────────────┘      └───────────────────┘   │
    └────────────────────┴──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
    ```
    
    Sidebar content order: brand and collapse control in an aligned header, read-only household context, Workspace links (Dashboard, Chores, Expenses, Agreements), then Settings/About utilities and a compact local-only note in the footer. The collapsed rail keeps its own expand control and centered icons with tooltips; do not place desktop collapse/expand controls in the content header. Mobile uses a menu trigger in the content header and a close control in the Sheet header. Active page has a pale blue tint and a narrow left accent or colored text (not both aggressively). Use Lucide icons for navigation.
    
    Profile switcher lives in the top bar: avatar + name + chevron. Dropdown lists all **active** roommates and includes a small explanation `Switch demo perspective`. It must never look like secure sign-in.
    
    ### Tablet (640–1023 px)
    
    Collapse sidebar to narrow icon rail (optional) or an accessible slide-over menu. Preserve title, breadcrumbs and switcher. Content grid may become 2 columns or stacked depending on available width.
    
    ### Phone (<640 px)
    
    Prefer a top bar with compact RoomieOS mark, current roommate switcher, and accessible navigation trigger. Navigation may be a drawer or compact bottom navigation; choose whichever is easier to implement reliably. Cards stack into one column. Forms open as full-width sheets or generous dialogs. **Never let the whole page overflow horizontally.**
    
    
    ### Expense implementation — 23–24 November 2026
    
    - Added pure integer-cent parsing/formatting, equal and largest-remainder percentage allocation, open-expense netting and deterministic largest-balance advisory transfers. BigInt intermediates protect cent arithmetic; persisted money remains safe integer numbers. Exact display formatting retains the final cent even at the safe integer maximum. Runtime validation and form previews share the same split functions; unsafe aggregate balances are rejected before publication/persistence.
    - Added expense CRUD, category/date/notes fields, multiple payer rows, participant chips and equal/percentage/fixed modes with exact per-person previews. Payer and share totals validate independently, including currency discrepancies and duplicate-role guards. Single-payer totals follow amount edits only while the contribution still matches the prior total; custom/multi-payer contributions must be rebalanced explicitly.
    - Added desktop tables and tablet/phone cards, contribution/share details, personal net position and Who owes whom. Whole-expense settlement requires explicit confirmation of resolution outside RoomieOS; reopening restores the record's balance effect. No pairwise/partial settlement or payment integration is implied. Delete requires confirmation.
    - Historical archived roles remain visible/editable while settled. New selections require active roommates; reopening an expense involving archived roommates requires their restoration. Currency remains locked by settled records. Failed saves retain facts, balances and form drafts; real same-browser cross-tab changes disable stale form saves until explicit reload.

    import { House } from "lucide-react";
    
    export function Wordmark({
      compact = false,
      iconOnly = false,
    }: {
      compact?: boolean;
      iconOnly?: boolean;
    }) {
      return (
        <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-2.5 text-base font-semibold tracking-tight">
          <span className="flex size-8 items-center justify-center rounded-lg bg-ink text-white">
            <House aria-hidden="true" className="size-[18px]" />
          </span>
          <span
            className={
              iconOnly ? "sr-only" : compact ? "sr-only sm:not-sr-only" : ""
            }
          >
            RoomieOS
          </span>
        </span>
      );
    }

    test("navigation adapts, collapsed labels have tooltips, and perspective selection works by keyboard", async ({
      page,
    }, testInfo) => {
      await page.addInitScript((state) => {
        if (localStorage.getItem("roomieos:v1") === null)
          localStorage.setItem("roomieos:v1", JSON.stringify(state));
      }, createSampleHousehold(new Date()));
      await page.goto("/");
      const original = await page.evaluate(() =>
        localStorage.getItem("roomieos:v1"),
      );
      if (page.viewportSize()!.width >= 1024) {
        const sidebar = page.locator('[data-slot="sidebar-container"]');
        await expect(
          sidebar.getByRole("button", { name: "Collapse sidebar", exact: true }),
        ).toBeVisible();
        await expect(
          page
            .locator("header")
            .getByRole("button", { name: /Collapse sidebar|Expand sidebar/ }),
        ).toHaveCount(0);
        await page.screenshot({
          animations: "disabled",
          path: testInfo.outputPath("sidebar-expanded.png"),
          fullPage: true,
        });
        const originalViewport = page.viewportSize()!;
        await page.setViewportSize({ width: originalViewport.width, height: 600 });
        const footer = page.locator('[data-slot="sidebar-footer"]');
        const footerBox = await footer.boundingBox();
        expect(footerBox!.y + footerBox!.height).toBeLessThanOrEqual(600);
        await expect(
          footer.getByRole("button", { name: "About this prototype", exact: true }),
        ).toBeInViewport();
        await page.setViewportSize(originalViewport);
        await sidebar
          .getByRole("button", { name: "Collapse sidebar", exact: true })
          .click();
        await expect(
          page.locator('[data-slot="sidebar"][data-state="collapsed"]'),
        ).toBeVisible();
        await expect(page.locator('[data-slot="sidebar-gap"]')).toHaveCSS(

        await page.getByRole("link", { name: "Chores", exact: true }).hover();
        await expect(
          page.getByRole("tooltip", { name: "Chores", exact: true }),

    "use client";
    import { CheckIcon, ChevronRightIcon } from "lucide-react";
    
    import * as React from "react";
    import { Menu as MenuPrimitive } from "@base-ui/react/menu";
    import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";
    
    function DropdownMenu({ ...props }: MenuPrimitive.Root.Props) {
      return <MenuPrimitive.Root data-slot="dropdown-menu" {...props} />;
    }
    
    function DropdownMenuPortal({ ...props }: MenuPrimitive.Portal.Props) {
      return <MenuPrimitive.Portal data-slot="dropdown-menu-portal" {...props} />;
    }
    
    function DropdownMenuTrigger({ ...props }: MenuPrimitive.Trigger.Props) {
      return <MenuPrimitive.Trigger data-slot="dropdown-menu-trigger" {...props} />;
    }
    
    function DropdownMenuContent({
      align = "start",
      alignOffset = 0,
      side = "bottom",
      sideOffset = 4,
      className,
      ...props
    }: MenuPrimitive.Popup.Props &
      Pick<
        MenuPrimitive.Positioner.Props,
        "align" | "alignOffset" | "side" | "sideOffset"
      >) {
      return (
        <MenuPrimitive.Portal>
          <MenuPrimitive.Positioner
            className="isolate z-50 outline-none"
            align={align}
            alignOffset={alignOffset}
            side={side}
            sideOffset={sideOffset}
          >
            <MenuPrimitive.Popup
              data-slot="dropdown-menu-content"
              className={cn(

      }

    import type { ComponentProps } from "react";
    import { Button as Primitive, buttonVariants } from "./primitives/button";
    import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";
    type Variant = "primary" | "secondary" | "ghost" | "danger";
    const variants = {
      primary: "default",
      secondary: "outline",
      ghost: "ghost",
      danger: "destructive",
    } as const;
    export function buttonStyles(
      variant: Variant = "primary",
      className = "",
      size: "default" | "icon" = "default",
    ) {
      return cn(
        buttonVariants({ variant: variants[variant], size }),
        "min-h-11 gap-2 rounded-lg",
        size === "icon" ? "size-11" : "h-auto px-4 py-2.5",
        variant === "danger" && "bg-danger text-white hover:bg-danger/90",
        className,
      );
    }
    export function Button({
      variant = "primary",
      size = "default",
      className,
      type = "button",
      ...props
    }: Omit<ComponentProps<typeof Primitive>, "variant" | "size" | "className"> & {
      className?: string;
      variant?: Variant;
      size?: "default" | "icon";
    }) {
      return (
        <Primitive
          type={type}
          variant={variants[variant]}
          size={size}
          className={buttonStyles(variant, className, size)}
          {...props}
        />
      );
    }
    import { Button as ButtonPrimitive } from "@base-ui/react/button";
    import { cva, type VariantProps } from "class-variance-authority";
    import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";
    
    const buttonVariants = cva(
      "group/button inline-flex shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-lg border border-transparent bg-clip-padding text-sm font-medium whitespace-nowrap transition-all outline-none select-none focus-visible:border-ring focus-visible:ring-3 focus-visible:ring-ring/50 active:not-aria-[haspopup]:translate-y-px disabled:pointer-events-none disabled:opacity-50 aria-invalid:border-destructive aria-invalid:ring-3 aria-invalid:ring-destructive/20 dark:aria-invalid:border-destructive/50 dark:aria-invalid:ring-destructive/40 [&_svg]:pointer-events-none [&_svg]:shrink-0 [&_svg:not([class*='size-'])]:size-4",
      {
        variants: {
          variant: {
            default: "bg-primary text-primary-foreground hover:bg-primary/80",
            outline:
              "border-border bg-background hover:bg-subtle hover:text-ink aria-expanded:bg-subtle aria-expanded:text-ink dark:border-input dark:bg-input/30 dark:hover:bg-input/50",
            secondary:
              "bg-secondary text-secondary-foreground hover:bg-[color-mix(in_oklch,var(--secondary),var(--foreground)_5%)] aria-expanded:bg-secondary aria-expanded:text-secondary-foreground",
            ghost:
              "hover:bg-subtle hover:text-ink aria-expanded:bg-subtle aria-expanded:text-ink dark:hover:bg-subtle/50",
            destructive:
              "bg-destructive/10 text-destructive hover:bg-destructive/20 focus-visible:border-destructive/40 focus-visible:ring-destructive/20 dark:bg-destructive/20 dark:hover:bg-destructive/30 dark:focus-visible:ring-destructive/40",
            link: "text-primary underline-offset-4 hover:underline",
          },
          size: {
            default:
              "h-8 gap-1.5 px-2.5 has-data-[icon=inline-end]:pr-2 has-data-[icon=inline-start]:pl-2",
            xs: "h-6 gap-1 rounded-[min(var(--radius-md),10px)] px-2 text-xs in-data-[slot=button-group]:rounded-lg has-data-[icon=inline-end]:pr-1.5 has-data-[icon=inline-start]:pl-1.5 [&_svg:not([class*='size-'])]:size-3",
            sm: "h-7 gap-1 rounded-[min(var(--radius-md),12px)] px-2.5 text-[0.8rem] in-data-[slot=button-group]:rounded-lg has-data-[icon=inline-end]:pr-1.5 has-data-[icon=inline-start]:pl-1.5 [&_svg:not([class*='size-'])]:size-3.5",
            lg: "h-9 gap-1.5 px-2.5 has-data-[icon=inline-end]:pr-2 has-data-[icon=inline-start]:pl-2",

        },

    function Button({

      variant = "default",
      size = "default",
      ...props
    }: ButtonPrimitive.Props & VariantProps<typeof buttonVariants>) {
      return (

    export { Button, buttonVariants };
    import { getDialog } from "./ui-controls";
    import { expect, test, type Page } from "@playwright/test";
    import { createSampleHousehold } from "../../src/domain/sample";
    
    test.beforeEach(async ({ page }) => {
      await page.addInitScript((state) => {
        if (localStorage.getItem("roomieos:v1") === null)
          localStorage.setItem("roomieos:v1", JSON.stringify(state));
      }, createSampleHousehold(new Date()));
    });
    
    async function openNavigationIfNeeded(page: Page) {
      const trigger = page.getByRole("button", { name: "Open navigation" });
      if (page.viewportSize()!.width < 1024) {
        await trigger.click();
        await expect(
          getDialog(page, { name: "Navigation", exact: true }),
        ).toBeVisible();
      }
    }
    
    async function expectNoPageOverflow(page: Page) {
      const overflow = await page.evaluate(
        () => document.documentElement.scrollWidth > window.innerWidth,
      );
      expect(overflow).toBe(false);
    }
    
    test("all household routes navigate, reload and fit the viewport", async ({
      page,
    }, testInfo) => {
      const errors: string[] = [];
      page.on("pageerror", (error) => errors.push(error.message));
      // React hydration mismatches can be console errors without a thrown page error.
      page.on("console", (message) => {
        if (message.type() === "error") errors.push(message.text());
      });
      await page.goto("/");
      await expect(page.getByRole("heading", { level: 1 })).toHaveText(
        "Maple House",
      );
      await expectNoPageOverflow(page);
      await page.screenshot({
        path: testInfo.outputPath("dashboard.png"),
        fullPage: true,
      });
    
      for (const [label, heading, path] of [
        ["Chores", "Chores", "/chores/"],
        ["Expenses", "Expenses", "/expenses/"],
        ["Agreements", "House agreements", "/agreements/"],
        ["Settings", "Settings", "/settings/"],
        ["Dashboard", "Maple House", "/"],
      ]) {
        await openNavigationIfNeeded(page);
        await page
          .getByRole("navigation", { name: "Main navigation" })
          .filter({ visible: true })
          .getByRole("link", { name: label, exact: true })
          .click();
        await expect(page).toHaveURL(new RegExp(`${path}$`));
        await expect(
          getDialog(page, { name: "Navigation", exact: true }),
        ).not.toBeVisible();
        await expect(page.getByRole("heading", { level: 1 })).toHaveText(heading);
        await page.reload();
        await expect(page.getByRole("heading", { level: 1 })).toHaveText(heading);
        await expectNoPageOverflow(page);
      }
      // Exercise a client handler after the final reload before checking diagnostics.
      await openNavigationIfNeeded(page);
      await page
        .getByRole("button", { name: "About this prototype", exact: true })
        .filter({ visible: true })
        .click();
      await expect(
        getDialog(page, { name: "About RoomieOS", exact: true }),
      ).toBeVisible();
      expect(errors).toEqual([]);
    });
    
    test("about dialog traps keyboard focus, closes with Escape and restores focus", async ({
      page,
    }) => {
      await page.goto("/");
      await openNavigationIfNeeded(page);
      await page
        .getByRole("button", { name: "About this prototype", exact: true })
        .filter({ visible: true })
        .click();
      const dialog = getDialog(page, {
        name: "About RoomieOS",
        exact: true,
      });
      const close = dialog.getByRole("button", { name: "Close About RoomieOS" });
      await expect(dialog).toBeVisible();
      await expect(close).toBeFocused();
      await page.keyboard.press("Tab");
      await expect(close).toBeFocused();
      await page.keyboard.press("Shift+Tab");
      await page.keyboard.press("Escape");
      await expect(dialog).not.toBeVisible();
      const expectedFocus =
        page.viewportSize()!.width >= 1024
          ? page
              .getByRole("button", { name: "About this prototype", exact: true })
              .filter({ visible: true })
          : page.getByRole("button", { name: "Open navigation" });
      await expect(expectedFocus).toBeFocused();
    });
    
    test("saved households bypass welcome without replacing their data", async ({
      page,
    }) => {
      await page.goto("/");
      await expect(page.getByRole("heading", { level: 1 })).toHaveText(
        "Maple House",
      );
      const saved = await page.evaluate(() => localStorage.getItem("roomieos:v1"));
      await page.goto("/welcome/");
      await expect(page).toHaveURL(/\/$/);
      await expect(page.getByRole("heading", { level: 1 })).toHaveText(
        "Maple House",
      );
      await page.reload();
      await expect(page.getByRole("heading", { level: 1 })).toHaveText(
        "Maple House",
      );
      expect(await page.evaluate(() => localStorage.getItem("roomieos:v1"))).toBe(
        saved,
      );
    });
    
    test("navigation drawer closes with Escape and returns focus", async ({
      page,
    }) => {
      test.skip(
        test.info().project.name === "desktop",
        "Desktop uses the sidebar.",
      );
      await page.goto("/");
      const trigger = page.getByRole("button", { name: "Open navigation" });
      await trigger.click();
      await expect(page.locator('[aria-label="Open navigation"]')).toHaveAttribute(
        "aria-expanded",
        "true",
      );
      await page.keyboard.press("Escape");
      await expect(
        getDialog(page, { name: "Navigation", exact: true }),
      ).not.toBeVisible();
      await expect(trigger).toBeFocused();
      await expect(trigger).toHaveAttribute("aria-expanded", "false");
    });

    @@ -143 +143,66 @@
     });
    +
    +test("perspective menu contains long names and scrolls a full roster without changing shared records", async ({
    +  page,
    +}, testInfo) => {
    +  const state = createSampleHousehold(new Date());
    +  state.household.name = "A".repeat(60);
    +  state.members[0].name = "A".repeat(40);
    +  for (let index = 3; index < 20; index++)
    +    state.members.push({
    +      id: `roommate-${index}`,
    +      name: `Roommate ${index} ${"B".repeat(28)}`,
    +      active: true,
    +      order: index,
    +      avatar: { kind: "initials" },
    +    });
    +  state.members.push({
    +    id: "archived",
    +    name: "Archived roommate",
    +    active: false,
    +    order: 20,
    +    avatar: { kind: "initials" },
    +  });
    +  await page.addInitScript((saved) => {
    +    if (!localStorage.getItem("roomieos:v1"))
    +      localStorage.setItem("roomieos:v1", JSON.stringify(saved));
    +  }, state);
    +  await page.goto("/chores/");
    +  const trigger = page.getByRole("button", { name: "Switch demo perspective" });
    +  await trigger.click();
    +  const menu = page.getByRole("menu");
    +  await expect(menu.getByRole("menuitemradio")).toHaveCount(20);
    +  await expect(menu.getByText("Archived roommate")).toHaveCount(0);
    +  const box = (await menu.boundingBox())!;
    +  expect(box.x).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(0);
    +  expect(box.x + box.width).toBeLessThanOrEqual(page.viewportSize()!.width);
    +  expect(box.y + box.height).toBeLessThanOrEqual(page.viewportSize()!.height);
    +  await expect(menu.getByText(/saved in this browser only/)).toBeInViewport();
    +  await page.screenshot({
    +    path: testInfo.outputPath("perspective-menu-full-roster.png"),
    +    animations: "disabled",
    +  });
    +  await page.keyboard.press("Escape");
    +  await expect(trigger).toBeFocused();
    +  expect(await page.evaluate(() => localStorage.getItem("roomieos:v1"))).toBe(
    +    JSON.stringify(state),
    +  );
    +  await trigger.focus();
    +  await page.keyboard.press("ArrowDown");
    +  await page.keyboard.press("End");
    +  await expect(menu.getByRole("menuitemradio").last()).toBeInViewport();
    +  await page.keyboard.press("Enter");
    +  await expect(menu).not.toBeVisible();
    +  await expect(trigger).toHaveAttribute("data-value", "roommate-19");
    +  await page.reload();
    +  await expect(trigger).toHaveAttribute("data-value", "roommate-19");
    +  const saved = await page.evaluate(() =>
    +    JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem("roomieos:v1")!),
    +  );
    +  expect(saved).toEqual({ ...state, selectedMemberId: "roommate-19" });
    +  expect(
    +    await page.evaluate(
    +      () => document.documentElement.scrollWidth > window.innerWidth,
    +    ),
    +  ).toBe(false);
    +});

    +test("perspective menu contains long names and scrolls a full roster without changing shared records", async ({
    +  page,
    +}, testInfo) => {
    +  const state = createSampleHousehold(new Date());
    +  state.household.name = "A".repeat(60);
    +  state.members[0].name = "A".repeat(40);
    +  for (let index = 3; index < 20; index++)
    +    state.members.push({
    +      id: `roommate-${index}`,
    +      name: `Roommate ${index} ${"B".repeat(28)}`,
    +      active: true,
    +      order: index,
    +      avatar: { kind: "initials", value: "" },
    +    });
    +  state.members.push({
    +    id: "archived",
    +    name: "Archived roommate",
    +    active: false,
    +    order: 20,
    +    avatar: { kind: "initials", value: "" },
    +  });
    +  await page.addInitScript((saved) => {
    +    if (!localStorage.getItem("roomieos:v1"))
    +      localStorage.setItem("roomieos:v1", JSON.stringify(saved));
    +  }, state);
    +  await page.goto("/chores/");
    +  const trigger = page.getByRole("button", { name: "Switch demo perspective" });
    +  await trigger.click();
    +  const menu = page.getByRole("menu");
    +  await expect(menu.getByRole("menuitemradio")).toHaveCount(20);
    +  await expect(menu.getByText("Archived roommate")).toHaveCount(0);
    +  const box = (await menu.boundingBox())!;
    +  expect(box.x).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(0);
    +  expect(box.x + box.width).toBeLessThanOrEqual(page.viewportSize()!.width);
    +  expect(box.y + box.height).toBeLessThanOrEqual(page.viewportSize()!.height);
    +  await expect(menu.getByText(/saved in this browser only/)).toBeInViewport();
    +  await page.screenshot({
    +    path: testInfo.outputPath("perspective-menu-full-roster.png"),

    +  await page.keyboard.press("End");

    "use client";
    
    import {
      createContext,
      useContext,
      useEffect,
      useState,
      useSyncExternalStore,
      type ReactNode,
    } from "react";
    import {
      createHouseholdStore,
      initialSnapshot,
      STORAGE_KEY,
      type HouseholdStore,
    } from "./household-store";
    
    const HouseholdContext = createContext<HouseholdStore | null>(null);
    
    export function HouseholdProvider({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
      const [store] = useState(() =>
        createHouseholdStore(() => window.localStorage),
      );
      useEffect(() => {
        store.initialize();
        const onStorage = (event: StorageEvent) => {
          if (event.key === STORAGE_KEY || event.key === null)
            store.checkForExternalChange();
        };
        const onFocus = () => store.checkForExternalChange();
        window.addEventListener("storage", onStorage);
        window.addEventListener("focus", onFocus);
        return () => {
          window.removeEventListener("storage", onStorage);
          window.removeEventListener("focus", onFocus);
        };
      }, [store]);
      return (
        <HouseholdContext.Provider value={store}>
          {children}
        </HouseholdContext.Provider>
      );
    }
    
    export function useHousehold() {
      const store = useContext(HouseholdContext);
      if (!store) throw new Error("HouseholdProvider is missing.");
      const snapshot = useSyncExternalStore(
        store.subscribe,
        store.getSnapshot,
        () => initialSnapshot,
      );
      return { ...snapshot, store };
    }
    
    export function useReadyHousehold() {
      const household = useHousehold();
      if (!household.state)
        throw new Error("This view requires a loaded household.");
      return { ...household, state: household.state };
    }
    import { Info } from "lucide-react";
    import type { ReactNode } from "react";
    
    export function InlineNotice({
      children,
      tone = "neutral",
    }: {
      children: ReactNode;
      tone?: "neutral" | "error";
    }) {
      return (
        <div
          role={tone === "error" ? "alert" : undefined}
          className={`flex items-start gap-3 rounded-[7px] px-4 py-3 text-sm ${tone === "error" ? "bg-danger-bg text-danger" : "bg-subtle text-muted"}`}
        >
          <Info aria-hidden="true" className="mt-0.5 size-4 shrink-0" />
          <div>{children}</div>
        </div>
      );
    }
    "use client";

            <main
              id="main-content"

    # RoomieOS implementation plan
    
    **Last updated:** 24 November 2026
    
    **Status:** Topbar and shadcn roommate-switcher polish implemented and verified. No deployment performed.
    
    **Current milestone:** Component review — topbar and roommate switcher complete.
    
    **Next milestone:** Milestone 6 — release verification and submission deliverables.
    
    **Delivery target:** Thursday, 26 November 2026; Friday, 27 November is the submission/fix buffer.
    
    ## 1. Purpose, authority and decisions
    
    Build a polished Next.js client-side prototype for ENTI 407. `docs/PRD.md` is authoritative for behavior; `docs/DESIGN.md` is authoritative for UI/UX. This document records approved architecture, sequencing and actual progress. It does not override those requirements.
    
    At plan approval, the repository contained documentation and a README, with no application scaffold. The foundation, household setup/persistence, chores, expenses, agreements and integrated dashboard are now implemented; see the progress record below for verification. Update this record after each working slice: check only completed work, record actual verification, and keep the current/next milestone accurate. Surface conflicts or new scope for user review rather than inventing requirements.
    
    ### Resolved requirements and defaults
    
    - **Approved Milestone 2 expansion:** The user raised the active-roommate limit from 5 to 20 and requested more avatar choices. The current limit is **2–20 active roommates**, with 40 named/grouped emoji presets plus initials. PRD and DESIGN version 1.1 now reflect this explicit user-approved change from the original 2–5 limit. Existing saved avatar values and `schemaVersion: 1` remain compatible.
    - **Backend conflict resolved:** The user selected the approved local-only scope. Use Next.js App Router without backend API routes, Server Actions, authentication or a database. Household operations run in the browser.
    - **History ordering:** Preserve completion events in append order; display newest first, as DESIGN specifies.
    - **Archived-member edge cases:** Historical records remain viewable and editable without losing member references. Before reopening an expense involving archived members, require their restoration. Before reopening a chore assigned to an archived member, require reassignment to an active member. Rotation can directly select the next active member.
    - **Design contrast:** Preserve the visual palette, but use sufficiently dark text variants where muted tokens fail contrast requirements.
    - **Priority:** All PRD must-haves remain MVP requirements. Defer photo uploads and decorative animation. The user explicitly approved dependency-free chore-card dragging during UI review on 24 November 2026.
    
    ## 2. Architecture and state
    ### Expense balance summary — 24 November 2026
    
    - User requested red negative net amounts when owing, green unsigned positive amounts when owed, and readable perspective-relative sentences. Keep zero neutral. Replace the selected-name/colon caption with a total naming the relevant counterparties, derived from existing simplified transfers.
    - Prioritize selected-roommate transfer sentences (You owe amount to name / name owes you amount); retain unrelated balances in Other household balances. Keep currency explicit, open-only arithmetic and outside-app whole-expense settlement semantics unchanged. No form, ledger, schema or action changes.
    - Verification: 122 unit/storage tests and all 45 Chromium expense/integration checks pass at 375/768/1280px. Lint, typecheck, source/test formatting, diff checks and production static build pass. New browser coverage checks red -CAD 60.00 split into CAD 40.00/CAD 20.00 sentences, green unsigned creditor amounts, neutral zero with unrelated household debts, profile switch/refresh and unchanged expense records. Existing rounding, settlement/reopen, storage-failure, archived-role and large-household regressions pass.
    - Reviewed the desktop summary screenshot; desktop/tablet/phone previews are saved under `/tmp/roomieos-expense-summary-review/`. Manual review: switch among a debtor, creditor and even roommate, then settle/reopen an expense and refresh. Keep household currency explicit. Forms, dashboard card presentation and domain calculations remain unchanged; Chromium only and no deployment performed.
    
    ### Selected-roommate-only expense summary — 24 November 2026
    
    - User requested only the selected roommate's transactions after reviewing the expense summary. Remove Other household balances from the balance panel and show only simplified transfers involving selectedMemberId. Use a personal zero-balance empty state even when other roommates have outstanding balances.
    - Keep integer-cent netting across the complete household before filtering suggested transfers; do not filter expense inputs to the calculation. Expense records, currency, settlement and saved state remain unchanged in this summary slice.
    - Verification: 122 unit/storage tests and all 24 Chromium expense checks pass at 375/768/1280px. Lint, typecheck, formatting, diff checks and production static build pass. Checks assert unrelated names/transfers are absent, personally even roommates see no suggested-transfer rows despite other household debts, profile switch/refresh works, and records/calculations/settlement remain unchanged. Manual review: switch among a debtor, creditor and even roommate and inspect the summary. Chromium only; no deployment performed.
    
    ### Topbar and roommate-switcher polish — 24 November 2026
    
    - User requested a modern topbar using shadcn, especially the roommate changer. Replace the labeled select and duplicate avatar with one shadcn/Base UI avatar/name menu trigger. Keep Demo perspective visible, show active roommates with avatars and a selected checkmark, and disclose browser-only saving in the menu footer.
    - Refine desktop breadcrumb hierarchy, icon treatment and header spacing while preserving the aligned 80px header and mobile navigation. Bound the menu to the viewport and 28rem height, scroll only the roster, wrap long menu names and truncate the trigger/household context. Keep the existing save-before-publish selection action, archived-member exclusion and stale-tab guards.
    - Verification: 122 unit/storage tests, lint, typecheck, source/test formatting and diff checks pass. `npm run build -- --webpack` passes and exports static pages. Default Turbopack builds fail when starting their CSS worker with Operation not permitted, including escalated retries; the supported Webpack build verifies production output without changing project configuration.
    - The broader shell/setup/navigation run passed 41 checks with one intentional desktop drawer skip and exposed a menu-label ambiguity in the 20-roommate case. Corrected the label and explicit close-on-selection behavior. The closing targeted Chromium run passes all 9 checks at 375/768/1280px, covering keyboard selection/focus, Escape without a write, refresh persistence, long names, archived-member exclusion, full-roster scrolling and the existing 20-roommate setup/archive/restore flow. Tests also assert shared records remain unchanged by perspective switching.
    - Reviewed desktop, tablet and phone screenshots, including the full roster menu. Manual review: open the topbar switcher, select another roommate with pointer or keyboard, refresh, and inspect mobile navigation and long-name truncation. Next slice: continue component review or Milestone 6 release verification. Chromium only; no deployment performed.

    docs/PRD.md:53:Authentication, invitations, cross-device or real-time collaboration, database/server, API integrations, notifications, payment processing, chat, AI features, scheduled/background chore recurrence, recurring expense automation, foreign exchange conversion, partial settlements, receipt uploads, audit-grade finances, native mobile apps, multiple households, analytics, or a full permission model.

    docs/PRD.md:127:- Users can read, edit and delete chores. Delete requires confirmation and removes that chore's completion history. Invalid input displays inline errors and saves nothing.
    docs/PRD.md:186:**FR-18 — Navigation, feedback and accessibility**
    docs/PRD.md:188:- All controls have readable labels, visible focus states, keyboard-operable dialogs and status actions, sufficient contrast, and clear form errors.
    docs/PRD.md:253:**Local images:** Restrict to compressed thumbnails. If storage quota is exceeded, show an error without discarding the current state; offer icon/initials as a fallback. Never depend on remote avatar URLs.

    export type CommandResult = { ok: true } | { ok: false; error: string };
    export type HouseholdSnapshot = {
      status: "loading" | "empty" | "ready" | "invalid" | "unavailable";
      state: AppState | null;
      error: string | null;
      conflict: boolean;
      notice: string | null;
    };
    export const initialSnapshot: HouseholdSnapshot = {
      status: "loading",
      state: null,
      error: null,
      conflict: false,
      notice: null,
    };
    
    export function createHouseholdStore(
      getStorage: () => StoragePort,
      makeId = () => crypto.randomUUID(),
      now = () => new Date(),
    ) {
      let snapshot = initialSnapshot;
      let lastRaw: string | null = null;
      const listeners = new Set<() => void>();
      const publish = (changes: Partial<HouseholdSnapshot>) => {
        snapshot = { ...snapshot, ...changes };
        listeners.forEach((listener) => listener());
      };
      const failure = (message: string): CommandResult => {
        publish({ error: message, notice: null });
        return { ok: false, error: message };
      };
      const messageFor = (error: unknown) =>
        error instanceof ZodError
          ? (error.issues[0]?.message ?? "Check the form and try again.")
          : error instanceof Error
            ? error.message
            : "The change could not be saved. Try again.";
    
        if (snapshot.status === "loading" || snapshot.status === "unavailable")
          return;
        try {
          if (getStorage().getItem(STORAGE_KEY) !== lastRaw)
            publish({ conflict: true, notice: null });
        } catch {
          publish({
            error:
              "Couldn't check browser storage. Saving will verify it again before applying any change.",
            notice: null,
          });
        }
      }
    
      return {
        getSnapshot: () => snapshot,
        subscribe: (listener: () => void) => {
          listeners.add(listener);
          return () => {
            listeners.delete(listener);
          };
        },
        initialize,
        dispatch,
        setup,
        sample,
        clear,
        checkForExternalChange,
        dismissFeedback: () => publish({ error: null, notice: null }),
      };
    }
    
    export type HouseholdStore = ReturnType<typeof createHouseholdStore>;

    - `InlineNotice`: a clear and calm explanation for constraints (e.g. currency locked).
    - `DataTable` or table-like layout: only as complex as needed; mobile cards.
    - `Modal/Sheet`: focused editing flow with clear save/cancel, not full-screen arbitrary complexity.
    
    **Interaction details:**
    - Hover: subtle gray; active: pale accent.
    - Loading: only where actual waiting exists; localStorage actions are immediate.
    - Saving: close modal and show toast like `Chore saved`; don't show fake spinners.
    - Destructive: always confirm, explain consequences, avoid accidentally deleting data.
    - Form errors: show beside fields; preserve typed values and focus first invalid field on attempted save.
    - Date: human-readable display (e.g. `Tue, Nov 24`) but store ISO date and compare local dates correctly.
    - Balance: tabular numerals; consistent currency code; never use color alone to indicate owed/owing.

      await expect(

      ).toBeVisible();
    }

      await expect(page).toHaveURL(/\/welcome\/$/);
      await page.getByRole("radio", { name: /Explore a sample household/ }).check();
      await selectControl(page.getByLabel("Household currency"), "USD");
      await page.getByRole("button", { name: "Continue with sample" }).click();
      await expect(page.getByRole("heading", { level: 1 })).toHaveText(
        "Maple House",
      );
    }
    async function enterEmpty(page: Page) {

      await page.getByRole("radio", { name: /Start from scratch/ }).check();
      await page.getByLabel("Household name").fill("Cedar House");
      await selectControl(page.getByLabel("Household currency"), "EUR");
      await page.getByLabel("Roommate 1 name").fill("Alex");
      await page.getByLabel("Roommate 2 name").fill("Morgan");

      await page.getByRole("button", { name: "Create my household" }).click();
      await expect(page.getByRole("heading", { level: 1 })).toHaveText(
        "Cedar House",
      );
    }
    async function addMember(page: Page, name: string) {
      await page.getByRole("button", { name: "Add roommate", exact: true }).click();
      const dialog = getDialog(page, {
        name: "Add roommate",
        exact: true,
      });
      await dialog.getByLabel("Roommate name").fill(name);
      await dialog
        .getByRole("button", { name: "Add roommate", exact: true })
        .click();
      await expect(dialog).not.toBeVisible();
    }

    ---
    title: use client
    description: Learn how to use the use client directive to render a component on the client.
    ---
    
    The `'use client'` directive declares an entry point for the components to be rendered on the **client side** and should be used when creating interactive user interfaces (UI) that require client-side JavaScript capabilities, such as state management, event handling, and access to browser APIs. This is a React feature.
    
    > **Good to know:**
    >
    > You do not need to add the `'use client'` directive to every file that contains Client Components. You only need to add it to the files whose components you want to render directly within Server Components. The `'use client'` directive defines the [server and client boundary](/docs/app/guides/server-and-client-boundary), and the components exported from such a file serve as entry points to the client.
    
    ## Usage
    
    To declare an entry point for the Client Components, add the `'use client'` directive **at the top of the file**, before any imports. The directive applies to the entire module and cannot be placed inside a component or function:
    
    ```tsx filename="app/components/counter.tsx" highlight={1} switcher
    'use client'
    
    import { useState } from 'react'
    
    export default function Counter() {
      const [count, setCount] = useState(0)
    
      return (
        <div>
          <p>Count: {count}</p>
          <button onClick={() => setCount(count + 1)}>Increment</button>
        </div>
      )
    }
    ```
    
    ```jsx filename="app/components/counter.js" highlight={1} switcher
    'use client'

    file changes: Completed · 1 changes
    Add: /home/csanchez/Projects/roomieos/tests/browser/feedback.spec.ts
    import { expect, test } from "@playwright/test";
    import { createSampleHousehold } from "../../src/domain/sample";
    import { selectControl } from "./ui-controls";
    
    test.beforeEach(async ({ page }) => {
      await page.addInitScript((state) => {
        if (!localStorage.getItem("roomieos:v1"))
          localStorage.setItem("roomieos:v1", JSON.stringify(state));
      }, createSampleHousehold(new Date()));
      await page.emulateMedia({ reducedMotion: "reduce" });
      await page.clock.install();
      await page.clock.pauseAt(new Date(Date.now() + 1000));
      await page.goto("/");
    });
    
    test("feedback sits bottom-right, expires after 3 seconds and restarts for repeated messages", async ({
      page,
    }, testInfo) => {
      const heading = page.getByRole("heading", { level: 1 });
      await expect(heading).toBeVisible();
      const originalHeading = await heading.boundingBox();
      const perspective = page.getByRole("button", { name: "Switch demo perspective" });
      await selectControl(perspective, "sam");
      const notice = page.getByRole("status").filter({ hasText: "Demo perspective changed." });
      await expect(notice).toBeVisible();
      const box = (await notice.locator("..").boundingBox())!;
      expect(page.viewportSize()!.width - box.x - box.width).toBeCloseTo(16, 0);
      expect(page.viewportSize()!.height - box.y - box.height).toBeCloseTo(16, 0);
      expect(await heading.boundingBox()).toEqual(originalHeading);
      await page.screenshot({ path: testInfo.outputPath("feedback-toast.png") });
      await page.clock.runFor(2500);
      await expect(notice).toBeVisible();
      await selectControl(perspective, "jordan");
      await page.clock.runFor(1000);
      await expect(notice).toBeVisible();
      await page.clock.runFor(1999);
      await expect(notice).toBeVisible();
      await page.clock.runFor(1);
      await expect(notice).not.toBeVisible();
      await selectControl(perspective, "avery");
      await expect(notice).toBeVisible();
      await page.getByRole("button", { name: "Dismiss message" }).click();
      await expect(notice).not.toBeVisible();
      await page.clock.runFor(3000);
      await expect(perspective).toHaveAttribute("data-value", "avery");
    });
    
    test("failed-save feedback expires while saved state stays intact", async ({ page }) => {
      const original = await page.evaluate(() => localStorage.getItem("roomieos:v1"));
      await page.evaluate(() => {
        Storage.prototype.setItem = () => {
          throw new DOMException("Storage is full", "QuotaExceededError");
        };
      });
      const perspective = page.getByRole("button", { name: "Switch demo perspective" });
      await selectControl(perspective, "sam");
      const error = page.getByRole("alert").filter({ hasText: /save|storage/i });
      await expect(error).toBeVisible();
      await expect(perspective).toHaveAttribute("data-value", "avery");
      await page.clock.runFor(3000);
      await expect(error).not.toBeVisible();
      expect(await page.evaluate(() => localStorage.getItem("roomieos:v1"))).toBe(original);
    });

    5-
    6-`devIndicators` allows you to configure the on-screen indicator that gives context about the current route you're viewing during development.
    7-
    8:Open `next.config.ts` and set `position` to choose where the indicator renders. The default is `bottom-left`.
    9-
    10-```ts filename="next.config.ts"
    11-import type { NextConfig } from 'next'
    12-
    13-const nextConfig: NextConfig = {
    14-  devIndicators: {
    15:    position: 'bottom-right', // 'bottom-left' | 'bottom-right' | 'top-left' | 'top-right'
    16-  },
    17-}
    18-
    19-export default nextConfig
    20-```
    21-
    22-To hide the indicator entirely, set `devIndicators` to `false`. Next.js will still surface any compile or runtime errors that were encountered.
    --
    68-| Version   | Changes                                                                                                                                             |
    69-| --------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
    70-| `v16.0.0` | `appIsrStatus`, `buildActivity`, and `buildActivityPosition` options have been removed.                                                             |
    71:| `v15.2.0` | Improved on-screen indicator with new `position` option. `appIsrStatus`, `buildActivity`, and `buildActivityPosition` options have been deprecated. |

    function ShellContent({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
      const { state, error, notice, store } = useReadyHousehold();
      // Each publication gets a fresh snapshot, even for repeated messages.
      const feedback = store.getSnapshot();
      const pathname = usePathname();
      const { open, setOpen, openMobile, setOpenMobile, isMobile } = useSidebar();
      const [aboutOpen, setAboutOpen] = useState(false);
      const currentPage =
        navigation.find(
          (item) => item.href === pathname || `${item.href}/` === pathname,
        )?.label ?? "RoomieOS";
      const drawerOpen = openMobile;
      const collapsed = !open && !isMobile;
    
      useEffect(() => {
        const desktop = window.matchMedia("(min-width: 1024px)");
        const closeOnDesktop = () => {
          if (desktop.matches) setOpenMobile(false);
        };
        desktop.addEventListener("change", closeOnDesktop);
        return () => desktop.removeEventListener("change", closeOnDesktop);
      }, [setOpenMobile]);
      useEffect(() => {
        setOpenMobile(false);
      }, [pathname, setOpenMobile]);
      useEffect(() => {
        if (!feedback.error && !feedback.notice) return;
        const timer = window.setTimeout(() => store.dismissFeedback(), 3000);
        return () => window.clearTimeout(timer);
      }, [feedback, store]);
    
      return (
        <>
          <a
            href="#main-content"
            className="fixed top-3 left-3 z-50 -translate-y-24 rounded-[7px] bg-accent px-4 py-3 text-sm font-medium text-white focus:translate-y-0"

    # RoomieOS implementation plan
    
    **Last updated:** 24 November 2026
    
    **Status:** Bottom-right feedback notifications with 3-second dismissal implemented and verified. No deployment performed.
    
    **Current milestone:** Component review — ephemeral feedback notifications complete.
    
    **Next milestone:** Milestone 6 — release verification and submission deliverables.
    
    **Delivery target:** Thursday, 26 November 2026; Friday, 27 November is the submission/fix buffer.
    
    ## 1. Purpose, authority and decisions
    
    Build a polished Next.js client-side prototype for ENTI 407. `docs/PRD.md` is authoritative for behavior; `docs/DESIGN.md` is authoritative for UI/UX. This document records approved architecture, sequencing and actual progress. It does not override those requirements.
    
    At plan approval, the repository contained documentation and a README, with no application scaffold. The foundation, household setup/persistence, chores, expenses, agreements and integrated dashboard are now implemented; see the progress record below for verification. Update this record after each working slice: check only completed work, record actual verification, and keep the current/next milestone accurate. Surface conflicts or new scope for user review rather than inventing requirements.
    
    ### Resolved requirements and defaults
    
    - **Approved Milestone 2 expansion:** The user raised the active-roommate limit from 5 to 20 and requested more avatar choices. The current limit is **2–20 active roommates**, with 40 named/grouped emoji presets plus initials. PRD and DESIGN version 1.1 now reflect this explicit user-approved change from the original 2–5 limit. Existing saved avatar values and `schemaVersion: 1` remain compatible.
    - **Backend conflict resolved:** The user selected the approved local-only scope. Use Next.js App Router without backend API routes, Server Actions, authentication or a database. Household operations run in the browser.
    - **History ordering:** Preserve completion events in append order; display newest first, as DESIGN specifies.
    - **Archived-member edge cases:** Historical records remain viewable and editable without losing member references. Before reopening an expense involving archived members, require their restoration. Before reopening a chore assigned to an archived member, require reassignment to an active member. Rotation can directly select the next active member.
    - **Design contrast:** Preserve the visual palette, but use sufficiently dark text variants where muted tokens fail contrast requirements.
    - **Priority:** All PRD must-haves remain MVP requirements. Defer photo uploads and decorative animation. The user explicitly approved dependency-free chore-card dragging during UI review on 24 November 2026.
    
    ## 2. Architecture and state
    
    Use Next.js App Router, TypeScript, Tailwind CSS and `lucide-react`, with static deployment on Vercel.
    
    ### Application structure
    
    - **Routes (`src/app`):** `/` for Dashboard, plus `/welcome`, `/chores`, `/expenses`, `/agreements` and `/settings`. A client initialization gate shows loading until storage is read, then presents setup, recovery or the requested screen.
    - **Shared UI (`src/components`, `src/styles`):** App shell, page header, avatar/picker, fields, status pills, accessible dialogs, confirmations, notices and empty states. Desktop sidebar; mobile navigation drawer. Tables become cards on phones; Kanban scrolls within its own region.
    - **Feature modules (`src/features`):** Setup, household, chores, expenses, agreements and dashboard. Keep form drafts local to their feature.
    - **Domain utilities (`src/domain`):** Types, runtime validation, money parsing/allocation, balances, rotation, date helpers and sample generation. These functions must not depend on React or browser storage.
    - **State layer (`src/state`):** One client provider with typed domain actions and shared selectors. Route changes must preserve the provider. Keep browser storage access behind a small adapter.
    
    Follow the PRD's `AppState` model with `schemaVersion: 1`. Use stable IDs and member ordering; retain archived members. Persist facts, selected profile and settings. Calculate balances, transfer suggestions, overdue indicators and dashboard counts from those facts.
    
    ### Important interfaces
    
    - Domain actions cover CRUD, status changes, rotation, archive/restore, profile selection and resets.
    - Commands return success or actionable validation/storage errors.
    - Storage exposes load, save and clear operations with explicit failure results.
    - Inject time and ID generation into domain operations where needed for deterministic tests.
    
    ### Financial rules
    
    - Parse currency input as decimal strings into integer cents; percentages into integer basis points.
    - Require safe integer amounts and reject inputs whose calculations exceed supported integer bounds.
    - Allocate equal-split remainder cents by stable roster order. Use largest remainder for percentages, with the same stable tie-breaker.
    - Validate payer and participant totals independently. Reject duplicate entries for the same member within each role.
    - Generate advisory transfers from open-expense nets, matching largest balances first with stable member-ID ties.
    - Settlement is a confirmed whole-expense marker for resolution outside the app, never an integrated payment action.
    
    ## 3. Persistence and recovery
    
    Use only `localStorage["roomieos:v1"]`.
    
    - Read storage once after client mount. Do not render household-dependent content or save defaults before initialization completes.
    - Distinguish absent data, valid data, malformed/unsupported data and unavailable storage.
    - Validate structure, references, roster constraints, dates and financial invariants on load and before persistence.
    - Generate sample data only after explicit selection or confirmed reset. Base dates on the local date at that moment.
    - For each change, calculate and validate the next state, persist it, then publish it to React. Show success only after saving succeeds.
    - If saving fails, preserve the prior household and the user's form input. Show a useful error and allow retry.
    - Invalid data remains untouched until the user confirms reset. Unsupported versions must not silently become sample data.
    - Reset demo replaces saved state only after confirmation. Start over clears it only after confirmation and successful storage removal.
    - On another tab's storage change, stop stale writes and offer reload before further editing.
    - Store calendar dates as `YYYY-MM-DD`; use timestamps for completion events. Recompute date-sensitive displays on focus and relevant interactions.
    
    ## 4. Milestones and implementation order
    
    Each milestone ends with relevant checks, a reviewable commit and a progress update here. Introduce polish with each feature rather than postponing the entire visual design until release. During intermediate milestones, hide unavailable actions instead of exposing dead buttons.
    "use client";
    
    import { useEffect, useState, type ReactNode } from "react";
    import { usePathname, useRouter } from "next/navigation";
    import { House } from "lucide-react";
    import { useHousehold } from "./household-provider";
    import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
    import { ConfirmDialog } from "@/components/ui/dialog";
    import { InlineNotice } from "@/components/ui/inline-notice";
    import { Wordmark } from "@/components/wordmark";
    
    export function HouseholdGate({
      children,
      welcome = false,
    }: {
      children: ReactNode;
      welcome?: boolean;
    }) {
      const { status, conflict, error, store } = useHousehold();
      const router = useRouter();
      const pathname = usePathname();
      const [confirmReset, setConfirmReset] = useState(false);
      const [resetError, setResetError] = useState<string>();
      const needsSetup = status === "empty" && !welcome;
      const hasHousehold = status === "ready" && welcome;
      useEffect(() => {
        if (needsSetup) router.replace("/welcome");
        if (hasHousehold) router.replace("/");
      }, [needsSetup, hasHousehold, router, pathname]);
    
      if (status === "loading" || needsSetup || hasHousehold)
        return (
          <main
            className="flex min-h-dvh items-center justify-center gap-3 px-5 text-sm text-muted"
            aria-busy="true"
          >
            <House aria-hidden="true" className="size-5" />
            <p>Opening your household…</p>
          </main>
        );
      if (status === "invalid" || status === "unavailable") {
        return (
          <main className="mx-auto flex min-h-dvh max-w-lg flex-col justify-center px-5 py-10">
            <div className="mb-10">
              <Wordmark />
            </div>
            <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold">
              {status === "invalid"
                ? "Saved data couldn't be opened"
                : "Browser storage is unavailable"}
            </h1>
            <div className="mt-5">
              <InlineNotice tone="error">{error}</InlineNotice>
            </div>
            {conflict && (
              <p className="mt-4 text-sm text-warning">
                Saved data changed in another tab. Retry before resetting.
              </p>
            )}
            <div className="mt-6 flex flex-wrap gap-3">
              <Button variant="secondary" onClick={() => store.initialize()}>
                Retry opening saved data
              </Button>
              {status === "invalid" && (
                <Button
                  variant="danger"
                  onClick={() => {
                    setResetError(undefined);
                    setConfirmReset(true);
                  }}
                >
                  Reset saved data
                </Button>
              )}
            </div>
            <ConfirmDialog
              open={confirmReset}
              onClose={() => setConfirmReset(false)}
              title="Reset saved data?"
              description="This permanently removes this browser's RoomieOS household, including chores, expenses and agreements. You will return to the sample or empty household choice."
              confirmLabel="Erase saved household"
              destructive
              error={resetError}
              onConfirm={() => {
                const result = store.clear();
                if (result.ok) setConfirmReset(false);
                else setResetError(result.error);
              }}
            />
          </main>
        );
      }
      return (
        <>
          {conflict && (
            <div
              className="border-b border-warning bg-warning-bg px-4 py-3 text-sm text-warning"
              role="alert"
            >
              <div className="mx-auto flex max-w-[1120px] flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3">
                <p>
                  This household changed in another tab. Reload before saving;
                  unsaved form entries will be lost when you reload.
                </p>
                <Button
                  variant="secondary"
                  onClick={() => window.location.reload()}
                >
                  Reload saved household
                </Button>
              </div>
            </div>
          )}
          {children}
        </>
      );
    }
    import type { Metadata } from "next";
    import "./globals.css";
    import { HouseholdProvider } from "@/state/household-provider";
    
    export const metadata: Metadata = {
      title: { default: "RoomieOS", template: "%s · RoomieOS" },
      description:
        "Your shared home, organized. A local household prototype for chores, expenses and house agreements.",
    };
    
    export default function RootLayout({
      children,
    }: Readonly<{ children: React.ReactNode }>) {
      return (
        <html lang="en">
          <body className="min-h-dvh text-base leading-relaxed antialiased">
            <HouseholdProvider>{children}</HouseholdProvider>
          </body>
        </html>
      );
    }

    import { Input } from "./primitives/input";
    import { Label } from "./primitives/label";
    import type { InputHTMLAttributes } from "react";
    
    export const controlStyles =
      "w-full min-w-0 rounded-[7px] border border-control bg-page px-3 py-2.5 text-base text-ink aria-invalid:border-danger disabled:bg-subtle";
    
    export function FormField({
      label,
      help,
      error,
      id,
      ...props
    }: InputHTMLAttributes<HTMLInputElement> & {
      id: string;
      label: string;
      help?: string;
      error?: string;
    }) {
      const descriptions =
        [help && `${id}-help`, error && `${id}-error`, props["aria-describedby"]]
          .filter(Boolean)
          .join(" ") || undefined;
      return (
        <div className="space-y-2">
          <Label htmlFor={id} className="block break-words text-sm font-medium">
            {label}
            {props.required && <span className="text-muted"> (required)</span>}
          </Label>
          <Input
            {...props}
            id={id}
            className={`${controlStyles} ${props.className ?? ""}`}
            aria-invalid={error ? true : props["aria-invalid"]}
            aria-describedby={descriptions}
          />
          {help && (
            <p id={`${id}-help`} className="text-sm text-muted">
              {help}
            </p>
          )}
          {error && (
            <p id={`${id}-error`} className="text-sm text-danger">
              {error}
            </p>
          )}
        </div>
      );
    }
