## Plan

# Simple landing page and demo login

## Summary

Introduce this flow:

**Landing (`/`) → Demo login (`/login`) → Household setup or dashboard (`/dashboard`)**

Keep the login entirely client-side and clearly identified as a simulation. Remember successful login across refreshes and browser restarts until explicitly logged out.

## Pages and navigation

- **Landing:** Use the existing RoomieOS branding, palette and shadcn components. Include a compact header, “Your shared home, organized” hero, one sentence describing the app, three brief feature summaries for chores, expenses and agreements, and a “Get started” button leading to `/login`. Include the browser-only prototype disclosure. No sidebar or fabricated testimonials/statistics.
- **Login:** Center a simple card with empty email/password fields, a password visibility toggle, “Log in” button and link back to the landing page. Explain that this is a demo login without real accounts.
- Accept exactly `some.email@example.com` and `n0t4passw0rd$`. Keep these values off-screen; do not prefill them. Show accessible inline validation for missing or incorrect credentials.
- Move the existing dashboard to `/dashboard`; update sidebar, brand links, redirects and workspace-return links accordingly. Leave other workspace URLs unchanged.
- Add “Log out of demo” to the sidebar footer, including the collapsed rail and mobile navigation. Logout returns to `/login` and preserves all household data.

## State and routing

- Add a small demo-login provider and client-side route gate. Store only a successful-login flag under a separate key, `roomieos:demo-login`; never store the entered email or password.
- Keep the existing `roomieos:v1` household payload and schema unchanged. This flag provides presentation flow only, with no authentication, security boundary, backend or network requests.
- Read the flag before rendering setup/workspace screens to prevent a flash of content. Public landing/login pages remain accessible regardless of household storage validity.
- After login, continue to the requested workspace route when entered through a deep link; otherwise open `/dashboard`. Existing household gating sends new visitors to `/welcome` for the explicit sample/empty choice.
- Logged-in visits to `/login` continue to the app. `/` always remains the landing page. “Get started” skips the form when already logged in.
- Synchronize logout across open tabs using storage events. If saving or removing the login flag fails, show an actionable error and keep the prior login state.
- Preserve existing household recovery, reset and start-over behavior. Household reset/start-over does not log the visitor out.
- During implementation, record the approved demo-login exception to the existing no-login entry flow in the project documents and update `PLAN.md`.

## Verification

- Test first visit, both setup choices, correct/incorrect credentials, password visibility, logout and login persistence after reload.
- Check direct workspace links, logged-in redirects, cross-tab logout and blocked storage.
- Verify logout never alters household records and that existing saved households open without reseeding.
- Update browser fixtures for the login flag and dashboard URL; run existing navigation, setup, persistence and feedback regressions.
- Review keyboard navigation, focus, labels and overflow at 375, 768 and 1280px. Run lint, TypeScript and the production build using the working Webpack compiler.

## Boundaries

No registration, password recovery, real accounts, remote assets or new dependencies. Implementation stays local; deployment is a separate release step.

## User

Implement the plan.

## Assistant

I’ll implement the landing page and persistent demo-login flow, then update navigation and verify that existing household data and setup behavior still work.

    # RoomieOS implementation plan
    
    **Last updated:** 24 November 2026
    
    **Status:** Bottom-right feedback notifications with 3-second dismissal implemented and verified. No deployment performed.
    
    **Current milestone:** Component review — ephemeral feedback notifications complete.
    
    **Next milestone:** Milestone 6 — release verification and submission deliverables.
    
    **Delivery target:** Thursday, 26 November 2026; Friday, 27 November is the submission/fix buffer.
    
    ## 1. Purpose, authority and decisions
    
    Build a polished Next.js client-side prototype for ENTI 407. `docs/PRD.md` is authoritative for behavior; `docs/DESIGN.md` is authoritative for UI/UX. This document records approved architecture, sequencing and actual progress. It does not override those requirements.
    
    At plan approval, the repository contained documentation and a README, with no application scaffold. The foundation, household setup/persistence, chores, expenses, agreements and integrated dashboard are now implemented; see the progress record below for verification. Update this record after each working slice: check only completed work, record actual verification, and keep the current/next milestone accurate. Surface conflicts or new scope for user review rather than inventing requirements.

    const navigation = [

      { href: "/agreements", label: "Agreements", Icon: BookOpen },
      { href: "/settings", label: "Settings", Icon: Settings },
    ];
    
    function Navigation({
      onNavigate,
      items = navigation.filter((item) => item.href !== "/settings"),
    }: {
      onNavigate?: () => void;
      items?: typeof navigation;
    }) {
      const pathname = usePathname();
      return (
        <SidebarMenu className="gap-1 group-data-[collapsible=icon]:items-center">
          {items.map(({ href, label, Icon }) => {
            const active = pathname === href || pathname === `${href}/`;
            return (
              <SidebarMenuItem key={href}>
                <SidebarMenuButton
                  render={<Link href={href} />}
                  isActive={active}
                  tooltip={label}
                  aria-label={label}
                  aria-current={active ? "page" : undefined}
                  onClick={onNavigate}
                  className="min-h-11 gap-3 rounded-lg px-3 text-muted hover:bg-hover hover:text-ink data-active:bg-selected data-active:text-accent group-data-[collapsible=icon]:justify-center group-data-[collapsible=icon]:gap-0 group-data-[collapsible=icon]:[&>span]:hidden [&_svg]:size-[18px]"
                >
                  <Icon aria-hidden="true" />
                  <span>{label}</span>
                </SidebarMenuButton>
              </SidebarMenuItem>
            );
          })}
        </SidebarMenu>
      );
    }
    
    export function AppShell({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
      return (
        <SidebarProvider>
          <ShellContent>{children}</ShellContent>
        </SidebarProvider>
      );
    }
    
    function ShellContent({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
      const { state, error, notice, store } = useReadyHousehold();
      // Each publication gets a fresh snapshot, even for repeated messages.

      const drawerOpen = openMobile;
      const collapsed = !open && !isMobile;
    
      useEffect(() => {
        const desktop = window.matchMedia("(min-width: 1024px)");
        const closeOnDesktop = () => {
          if (desktop.matches) setOpenMobile(false);
        };
        desktop.addEventListener("change", closeOnDesktop);
        return () => desktop.removeEventListener("change", closeOnDesktop);
      }, [setOpenMobile]);
      useEffect(() => {
        setOpenMobile(false);
      }, [pathname, setOpenMobile]);
      useEffect(() => {
        if (!feedback.error && !feedback.notice) return;
        const timer = window.setTimeout(() => store.dismissFeedback(), 3000);
        return () => window.clearTimeout(timer);
      }, [feedback, store]);
    
      return (

                  <X aria-hidden="true" className="size-4" />
                </Button>
              </div>
            )}
    
            <Dialog
              open={aboutOpen}
              onClose={() => setAboutOpen(false)}
              title="About RoomieOS"
              returnFocusId={isMobile ? "mobile-nav-toggle" : "about-prototype"}
              description="Your shared home, organized."
            >
              <div className="space-y-4 text-sm text-muted">
                <p>
                  RoomieOS is an ENTI 407 prototype for coordinating household
                  chores, shared expenses and house agreements.

                <p>
                  Coordinate chores, shared expenses and house agreements. Household

                <p>

                  outside RoomieOS. The app does not move money.

    ---
    name: sites
    description: Use Sites when the user wants a website built for them (including a landing page, portfolio, dashboard, portal, tracker, hub, game, event registration, or tool), requests changes to a website already built with Sites, wants to publish a website with Sites, or wants to manage Sites hosting.
    ---
    
    # Sites
    
    Take the requested website through the supported development and publishing workflow. Publish completed new Sites and edits unless the user requests local-only work or saving without deployment. New Sites start private; preserve an existing Site's audience unless the user requests a change. Native tool approvals apply without an additional conversational publishing gate.
    
    For hosting-only changes, use the relevant native Sites tool and confirm its returned state; source preparation is needed only for source changes or publication.
    
    ## Workflow
    
    `<plugin-root>` is the installed directory containing `scripts/` and `skills/`. Run helpers with absolute paths and literal arguments from the selected checkout. Keep credentials in session memory and pass them through stdin, never shell arguments, files, or output.
    
    ### 1. Select and open
    
    Default to a new Site unless a specific existing Site is identified.
    
    - **Existing hosted Site:** retain its `project_id` and audience, call `get_site` and `create_source_repository_write_credential`, and open with the [source helper](#source-helper) before editing. Omit `archivePath`; use an empty directory when source is missing, then the returned `checkout_path`.
    - **New Site:** create a unique, new empty project subdirectory in the task's workspace, normally `/workspace/sites/<slug>` in managed Linux. Default simple pages and browser-local interactions to buildless HTML/JS; use the supplied Vinext starter for server behavior. If the user requests another framework or implementation, or the task specifies a template to reuse, skip the Vinext starter and build directly with that choice. For hosting, follow the minimum deploy format under [Hosting manifest](#hosting-manifest).
    - **Local-only:** use the available checkout; skip registration, source synchronization, and publication.
    
    ### 2. Prepare and launch
    
    For new Vinext projects, run:
    
    ```text
    node <plugin-root>/scripts/project-setup.mjs
    ```
    
    This copies the starter and configures ignored checkout-local state. Helpers select `managed-linux` only for `SITES_MANAGED_LINUX_CONTAINER=1`, otherwise `portable`. For buildless HTML/JS, prepare `dist/` for `index.html` and assets; skip starter setup, installation, and build.
    
    For a retained template, add `--template-source <absolute-sanitized-source-directory>`; exclude Site identity, Git metadata, credentials, and runtime data. For explicitly requested Worker ESM, add `--starter worker-esm` and follow [its README](templates/worker-esm-starter/README.md). Use [Troubleshooting](references/troubleshooting.md#project-configuration) for profile repair or static-to-Worker migration.
    
    ---
    title: How to create a static export of your Next.js application
    nav_title: Static Exports
    description: Next.js enables starting as a static site or Single-Page Application (SPA), then later optionally upgrading to use features that require a server.
    ---
    
    {/* The content of this doc is shared between the app and pages router. You can use the `<PagesOnly>Content</PagesOnly>` component to add content that is specific to the Pages Router. Any shared content should not be wrapped in a component. */}
    
    Next.js enables starting as a static site or Single-Page Application (SPA), then later optionally upgrading to use features that require a server.
    
    When running `next build`, Next.js generates an HTML file per route. By breaking a strict SPA into individual HTML files, Next.js can avoid loading unnecessary JavaScript code on the client-side, reducing the bundle size and enabling faster page loads.
    
    Since Next.js supports this static export, it can be deployed and hosted on any web server that can serve HTML/CSS/JS static assets.
    
    ## Configuration
    
    To enable a static export, change the output mode inside `next.config.js`:
    
    ```js filename="next.config.js" highlight={5}
    /**
     * @type {import('next').NextConfig}
     */
    const nextConfig = {
      output: 'export',
    
      // Optional: Change links `/me` -> `/me/` and emit `/me.html` -> `/me/index.html`
      // trailingSlash: true,
    
      // Optional: Prevent automatic `/me` -> `/me/`, instead preserve `href`
      // skipTrailingSlashRedirect: true,
    
      // Optional: Change the output directory `out` -> `dist`
      // distDir: 'dist',
    }
    
    module.exports = nextConfig
    ```
    
    After running `next build`, Next.js will create an `out` folder with the HTML/CSS/JS assets for your application.
    
    <PagesOnly>
    
    You can utilize [`getStaticProps`](/docs/pages/building-your-application/data-fetching/get-static-props) and [`getStaticPaths`](/docs/pages/building-your-application/data-fetching/get-static-paths) to generate an HTML file for each page in your `pages` directory (or more for [dynamic routes](/docs/app/api-reference/file-conventions/dynamic-routes)).
    
    </PagesOnly>
    import type { ComponentProps } from "react";
    import { Button as Primitive, buttonVariants } from "./primitives/button";
    import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";
    type Variant = "primary" | "secondary" | "ghost" | "danger";
    const variants = {
      primary: "default",
      secondary: "outline",
      ghost: "ghost",
      danger: "destructive",
    } as const;
    export function buttonStyles(
      variant: Variant = "primary",
      className = "",
      size: "default" | "icon" = "default",
    ) {
      return cn(
        buttonVariants({ variant: variants[variant], size }),
        "min-h-11 gap-2 rounded-lg",
        size === "icon" ? "size-11" : "h-auto px-4 py-2.5",
        variant === "danger" && "bg-danger text-white hover:bg-danger/90",
        className,
      );
    }
    export function Button({
      variant = "primary",
      size = "default",
      className,
      type = "button",
      ...props
    }: Omit<ComponentProps<typeof Primitive>, "variant" | "size" | "className"> & {
      className?: string;
      variant?: Variant;
      size?: "default" | "icon";
    }) {
      return (
        <Primitive
          type={type}
          variant={variants[variant]}
          size={size}
          className={buttonStyles(variant, className, size)}
          {...props}
        />
      );
    }

        "use client";
        
        import { createContext, useContext, useEffect, useState, type ReactNode } from "react";
        import { usePathname, useRouter } from "next/navigation";
        
        const LOGIN_KEY = "roomieos:demo-login";
        type Status = "loading" | "signed-out" | "signed-in";
        const DemoLoginContext = createContext<{
          status: Status;
          error: string | null;
          login: (email: string, password: string) => boolean;
          logout: () => boolean;
        } | null>(null);
        
        export function demoDestination() {
          const next = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get("next")?.replace(/\/$/, "");
          return next && ["/dashboard", "/chores", "/expenses", "/agreements", "/settings", "/welcome"].includes(next)
            ? next : "/dashboard";
        }
        
        export function DemoLoginProvider({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
          const [status, setStatus] = useState<Status>("loading");
          const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
          useEffect(() => {
            function read() {
              try {
                setStatus(localStorage.getItem(LOGIN_KEY) === "true" ? "signed-in" : "signed-out");
              } catch {
                setStatus((previous) => previous === "loading" ? "signed-out" : previous);
                setError("Browser storage is unavailable. Allow site storage and try again.");
              }
            }
            read();
            const onStorage = (event: StorageEvent) => {
              if (event.key === LOGIN_KEY || event.key === null) read();
            };
            window.addEventListener("storage", onStorage);
            window.addEventListener("focus", read);
            return () => {
              window.removeEventListener("storage", onStorage);
              window.removeEventListener("focus", read);
            };
          }, []);
          function login(email: string, password: string) {
            if (email !== "some.email@example.com" || password !== "n0t4passw0rd$") {
              setError("The demo email or password is incorrect.");
              return false;
            }
            try {
              localStorage.setItem(LOGIN_KEY, "true");
              setError(null);
              setStatus("signed-in");
              return true;
            } catch {
              setError("Couldn't remember your demo login. Allow site storage and try again.");
              return false;
            }
          }
          function logout() {
            try {
              localStorage.removeItem(LOGIN_KEY);
              setError(null);
              setStatus("signed-out");
              return true;
            } catch {
              setError("Couldn't log out of the demo. Allow site storage and try again.");
              return false;
            }
          }
          return <DemoLoginContext.Provider value={{ status, error, login, logout }}>{children}</DemoLoginContext.Provider>;
        }
        
        export function useDemoLogin() {
          const value = useContext(DemoLoginContext);
          if (!value) throw new Error("DemoLoginProvider is missing.");
          return value;
        }
        
        export function DemoLoginGate({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
          const { status } = useDemoLogin();
          const pathname = usePathname();
          const router = useRouter();
          useEffect(() => {
            if (status === "signed-out") router.replace(`/login?next=${encodeURIComponent(pathname)}`);
          }, [status, pathname, router]);
          if (status !== "signed-in") return <main aria-busy="true" className="flex min-h-dvh items-center justify-center px-5 text-sm text-muted">Opening RoomieOS…</main>;
          return children;
        }
        EOF
        cat > src/features/demo-login/login-screen.tsx <<'EOF'
        "use client";
        
        import Link from "next/link";
        import { useEffect, useRef, useState, type FormEvent } from "react";
        import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
        import { Eye, EyeOff } from "lucide-react";
        import { Wordmark } from "@/components/wordmark";
        import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
        import { FormField } from "@/components/ui/form-field";
        import { demoDestination, useDemoLogin } from "./demo-login-provider";
        
        export function LoginScreen() {
          const { status, error, login } = useDemoLogin();
          const router = useRouter();
          const [visible, setVisible] = useState(false);
          const emailRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);
          useEffect(() => {
            if (status === "signed-in") router.replace(demoDestination());
          }, [status, router]);
          function submit(event: FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) {
            event.preventDefault();
            const data = new FormData(event.currentTarget);
            if (!login(String(data.get("email")), String(data.get("password")))) emailRef.current?.focus();
          }
          return (
            <main className="flex min-h-dvh flex-col items-center justify-center bg-subtle px-4 py-10">
              <Link href="/" aria-label="RoomieOS home" className="mb-8 rounded-lg"><Wordmark /></Link>
              <section aria-labelledby="login-title" className="w-full max-w-md rounded-2xl border border-line bg-page p-6 shadow-sm sm:p-8">
                <p className="mb-2 text-xs font-medium tracking-widest text-accent uppercase">Local prototype</p>
                <h1 id="login-title" className="text-2xl font-semibold tracking-tight">Welcome to your shared home</h1>
                <p className="mt-3 text-sm text-muted">Use the demo login to continue. This is a simulation, with no real accounts. Your household stays in this browser.</p>
                <form onSubmit={submit} className="mt-7 space-y-5">
                  <FormField ref={emailRef} id="login-email" name="email" label="Email" type="email" autoComplete="off" required aria-describedby={error ? "login-error" : undefined} />
                  <div className="relative">
                    <FormField id="login-password" name="password" label="Password" type={visible ? "text" : "password"} autoComplete="off" required className="pr-12" aria-describedby={error ? "login-error" : undefined} />
                    <Button variant="ghost" size="icon" aria-label={visible ? "Hide password" : "Show password"} aria-pressed={visible} onClick={() => setVisible(!visible)} className="absolute right-0 bottom-0">{visible ? <EyeOff aria-hidden="true" /> : <Eye aria-hidden="true" />}</Button>
                  </div>
                  {error && <p id="login-error" role="alert" className="text-sm text-danger">{error}</p>}
                  <Button type="submit" disabled={status === "loading" || status === "signed-in"} className="w-full">Log in</Button>
                </form>
              </section>
              <Link href="/" className="mt-6 rounded text-sm text-muted underline underline-offset-4">Back to home</Link>
            </main>
          );
        }

        import { expect, test, type Page } from "@playwright/test";
        import { createSampleHousehold } from "../../src/domain/sample";
        
        async function login(page: Page) {
          await page.getByLabel("Email", { exact: true }).fill("some.email@example.com");
          await page.getByLabel("Password", { exact: true }).fill("n0t4passw0rd$");
          await page.getByRole("button", { name: "Log in", exact: true }).click();
        }
        async function logoutButton(page: Page) {
          if (page.viewportSize()!.width < 1024)
            await page.getByRole("button", { name: "Open navigation" }).click();
          return page.getByRole("button", { name: "Log out of demo", exact: true }).filter({ visible: true });
        }
        
        test("landing, empty login and first-run setup are explicit", async ({ page }, testInfo) => {
          await page.goto("/");
          await expect(page.getByRole("heading", { level: 1 })).toHaveText("Your shared home,organized.");
          await expect(page.getByText(/Saved in this browser only/)).toBeVisible();
          await page.screenshot({ path: testInfo.outputPath("landing.png"), fullPage: true });
          await page.getByRole("link", { name: "Get started" }).click();
          await expect(page.getByLabel("Email", { exact: true })).toHaveValue("");
          const password = [REDACTED_SECRET]"Password", { exact: true });
          await expect(password).toHaveValue("");
          await expect(page.getByText("some.email@example.com", { exact: true })).toHaveCount(0);
          await page.getByLabel("Email", { exact: true }).fill("wrong@example.com");
          await password.fill("wrong-password");
          await page.getByRole("button", { name: "Show password" }).click();
          await expect(password).toHaveAttribute("type", "text");
          await page.getByRole("button", { name: "Hide password" }).click();
          await expect(password).toHaveAttribute("type", "password");
          await page.getByRole("button", { name: "Log in", exact: true }).click();
          await expect(page.getByRole("alert").filter({ hasText: /incorrect/ })).toBeVisible();
          expect(await page.evaluate(() => localStorage.getItem("roomieos:demo-login"))).toBeNull();
          await login(page);
          await expect(page).toHaveURL(/\/welcome\/$/);
          expect(await page.evaluate(() => localStorage.getItem("roomieos:v1"))).toBeNull();
          await expect(page.getByRole("radio", { name: /Explore/ })).not.toBeChecked();
          await expect(page.getByRole("radio", { name: /Start from scratch/ })).not.toBeChecked();
          await page.reload();
          await expect(page).toHaveURL(/\/welcome\/$/);
        });
        
        test("deep links, persistent login and cross-tab logout preserve the household", async ({ page, context }) => {
          const state = createSampleHousehold(new Date());
          await context.addInitScript((saved) => {
            if (!localStorage.getItem("roomieos:v1")) localStorage.setItem("roomieos:v1", JSON.stringify(saved));
          }, state);
          await page.goto("/expenses/");
          await expect(page).toHaveURL(/\/login\/\?next=/);
          await expect(page.getByRole("heading", { name: "Expenses", exact: true })).toHaveCount(0);
          await login(page);
          await expect(page).toHaveURL(/\/expenses\/$/);
          await page.reload();
          await expect(page.getByRole("heading", { name: "Expenses", exact: true })).toBeVisible();
          const other = await context.newPage();
          await other.goto("/login/");
          await expect(other).toHaveURL(/\/dashboard\/$/);
          await (await logoutButton(page)).click();
          await expect(page).toHaveURL(/\/login\//);
          await expect(other).toHaveURL(/\/login\//);
          expect(await page.evaluate(() => localStorage.getItem("roomieos:v1"))).toBe(JSON.stringify(state));
          await page.reload();
          await expect(page.getByRole("button", { name: "Log in", exact: true })).toBeVisible();
          await page.goto("/");
          await expect(page.getByRole("link", { name: "Get started" })).toBeVisible();
          await other.close();
        });
        
        test("login storage failures keep the form usable and logout failures retain access", async ({ page }) => {
          await page.addInitScript((state) => {
            if (!localStorage.getItem("roomieos:v1")) localStorage.setItem("roomieos:v1", JSON.stringify(state));
          }, createSampleHousehold(new Date()));
          await page.goto("/login/");
          await page.evaluate(() => {
            const original = Storage.prototype.setItem;
            Storage.prototype.setItem = function (key, value) {
              if (key === "roomieos:demo-login") throw new Error("blocked");
              return original.call(this, key, value);
            };
            (window as unknown as { allowLogin: () => void }).allowLogin = () => { Storage.prototype.setItem = original; };
          });
          await login(page);
          await expect(page.getByRole("alert").filter({ hasText: /remember your demo login/ })).toBeVisible();
          await page.evaluate(() => (window as unknown as { allowLogin: () => void }).allowLogin());
          await login(page);
          await expect(page).toHaveURL(/\/dashboard\/$/);
          await page.evaluate(() => {
            Storage.prototype.removeItem = () => { throw new Error("blocked"); };
          });
          await (await logoutButton(page)).click();
          await expect(page.getByRole("alert").filter({ hasText: /Couldn't log out/ })).toBeVisible();
          expect(await page.evaluate(() => localStorage.getItem("roomieos:demo-login"))).toBe("true");
        });
        EOF

    test("blocked reads show retry without overwriting the saved household", async ({
      page,
    }) => {
      await page.addInitScript((state) => {
        localStorage.setItem("roomieos:v1", JSON.stringify(state));
        const original = Storage.prototype.getItem;
        Object.assign(window, {
          allowRoomieosReads: () => {
            Storage.prototype.getItem = original;
          },
        });
        Storage.prototype.getItem = function (key) {
          if (key === "roomieos:v1")
            throw new DOMException("Blocked", "SecurityError");
          return original.call(this, key);
        };
      }, createSampleHousehold(new Date()));
      await page.goto("/dashboard/");
      await expect(
        page.getByRole("heading", { name: "Browser storage is unavailable" }),
      ).toBeVisible();
      await page.evaluate(() =>
        (
          window as unknown as { allowRoomieosReads: () => void }
        ).allowRoomieosReads(),
      );
      await page.getByRole("button", { name: "Retry opening saved data" }).click();
      await expect(page.getByRole("heading", { level: 1 })).toHaveText(
        "Maple House",
      );
      expect((await saved(page))!.members).toHaveLength(3);
    });

    async function seed(
      page: Page,
      path = "/agreements/",
      state = createSampleHousehold(new Date()),
    ) {
      await page.addInitScript((value) => {
        if (localStorage.getItem("roomieos:v1") === null)
          localStorage.setItem("roomieos:v1", JSON.stringify(value));
      }, state);
      await page.goto(path);
      await expect(page.getByRole("heading", { level: 1 })).toBeVisible();
    }
    function empty() {
      const state = createSampleHousehold(new Date());
      state.chores = [];
      state.expenses = [];
      ).toBeVisible();
    });

      test.use({ timezoneId: "America/Edmonton" });
      test("dashboard overdue labels update on focus after local midnight", async ({
        page,
      }) => {
        const time = new Date(
          await page.evaluate(() => new Date(2026, 10, 24, 23, 59).toISOString()),
        );
        await page.clock.install({ time });
        const state = createSampleHousehold(new Date(2026, 10, 24, 12));
        state.chores[0].dueDate = "2026-11-24";
        await seed(page, "/", state);
        await expect(page.getByText("Overdue", { exact: true })).toHaveCount(0);
        await page.clock.setSystemTime(new Date(time.getTime() + 120_000));
        await page.evaluate(() => window.dispatchEvent(new Event("focus")));
        await expect(page.getByText("Overdue", { exact: true })).toHaveCount(1);
        await expect(summaryCard(page, "Household at a glance")).toContainText(
          "Pending chores3",
        );
      });
    });

    "use client";
    
    import {
      createContext,
      useContext,
      useEffect,
      useState,
      type ReactNode,
    } from "react";
    import { usePathname, useRouter } from "next/navigation";
    
    const LOGIN_KEY = "roomieos:demo-login";
    type Status = "loading" | "signed-out" | "signed-in";
    const DemoLoginContext = createContext<{
      status: Status;
      error: string | null;
      login: (email: string, password: string) => boolean;
      logout: () => boolean;
    } | null>(null);
    
    export function demoDestination() {
      const next = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search)
        .get("next")
        ?.replace(/\/$/, "");
      return next &&
        [
          "/dashboard",
          "/chores",
          "/expenses",
          "/agreements",
          "/settings",
          "/welcome",
        ].includes(next)
        ? next
        : "/dashboard";
    }
    
    export function DemoLoginProvider({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
      const [status, setStatus] = useState<Status>("loading");
      const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
      useEffect(() => {
        function read() {
          try {
            setStatus(
              localStorage.getItem(LOGIN_KEY) === "true"
                ? "signed-in"
                : "signed-out",
            );
          } catch {
            setStatus((previous) =>
              previous === "loading" ? "signed-out" : previous,
            );
            setError(
              "Browser storage is unavailable. Allow site storage and try again.",
            );
          }
        }
        read();
        const onStorage = (event: StorageEvent) => {
          if (event.key === LOGIN_KEY || event.key === null) read();
        };
        window.addEventListener("storage", onStorage);
        window.addEventListener("focus", read);
        return () => {
          window.removeEventListener("storage", onStorage);
          window.removeEventListener("focus", read);
        };
      }, []);
      function login(email: string, password: string) {
        if (email !== "some.email@example.com" || password !== "n0t4passw0rd$") {
          setError("The demo email or password is incorrect.");
          return false;
        }
        try {
          localStorage.setItem(LOGIN_KEY, "true");
          setError(null);
          setStatus("signed-in");
          return true;
        } catch {
          setError(
            "Couldn't remember your demo login. Allow site storage and try again.",
          );
          return false;
        }
      }
      function logout() {
        try {
          localStorage.removeItem(LOGIN_KEY);
          setError(null);
          setStatus("signed-out");
          return true;
        } catch {
          setError(
            "Couldn't log out of the demo. Allow site storage and try again.",
          );
          return false;
        }
      }
      return (
        <DemoLoginContext.Provider value={{ status, error, login, logout }}>
          {children}
        </DemoLoginContext.Provider>
      );
    }
    
    export function useDemoLogin() {
      const value = useContext(DemoLoginContext);
      if (!value) throw new Error("DemoLoginProvider is missing.");
      return value;
    }
    
    export function DemoLoginGate({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
      const { status } = useDemoLogin();
      const pathname = usePathname();
      const router = useRouter();
      useEffect(() => {
        if (status === "signed-out")
          router.replace(`/login?next=${encodeURIComponent(pathname)}`);
      }, [status, pathname, router]);
      if (status !== "signed-in")
        return (
          <main
            aria-busy="true"
            className="flex min-h-dvh items-center justify-center px-5 text-sm text-muted"
          >
            Opening RoomieOS…
          </main>
        );
      return children;
    }

     # RoomieOS — Design System & Interface Specification
    
    +**Amended 24 November 2026 — approved demo entry flow:** `/` is a public landing page; `/login` is a clearly labeled client-only demo login. Successful login persists until explicit logout using a separate `roomieos:demo-login` localStorage flag. The dashboard moves to `/dashboard`; setup and workspace routes require this simulated login. Credentials are not displayed or prefilled. Logout preserves household data. This supersedes the earlier no-login entry flow only; no real accounts, authentication service or backend is introduced.
    +
    +
     **Version:** 1.1 · **Design direction:** Calm, editorial workspace inspired by Notion (not a clone)

     **Implementation:** Next.js + TypeScript + Tailwind CSS; shadcn/ui with Base UI accessible primitives and Lucide icons; deployed on Vercel

        ### Landing page and persistent demo login — 24 November 2026
        
        - Implemented the approved public landing page at `/` with RoomieOS branding, a short hero, three feature summaries and working login/Get started links. Added a centered shadcn-based demo login at `/login`, with empty labeled required fields, a password visibility toggle, inline errors and no displayed credentials. Both pages disclose the local-only prototype.
        - Move Dashboard to `/dashboard`; update navigation, brand/return links and household redirects. Setup/workspace gates wait for initialization and redirect signed-out visitors to login, preserving allowlisted workspace deep links. The login page routes returning visitors directly into the app; the landing page stays public.
        - Add Log out of demo to expanded/collapsed/mobile navigation. Logout removes only the separate flag and propagates across tabs. Failed login writes or logout removals show actionable errors without changing the prior login state. Household schema, selected roommate and all saved records remain unchanged; existing recovery/reset/start-over flows stay intact. This is a simulated presentation gate, not real authentication.
